## V11

### bills_create

`v11.bills_create`

Create a new bill.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.elorus.api.v11.bills_create({
	body: {
		supplier: '3300000040000100',
		items: [],
	},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `demo` | `boolean` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  id?: string,
  custom_id?: string,
  representation?: string,
  status?: string,
  draft?: boolean,
  documenttype?: string | null,
  self_billed?: boolean,
  sequence?: string | null,
  reference?: string,
  date?: string,
  due_days?: number | null,
  branch?: string | null,
  supplier: string,
  supplier_display_name?: string,
  supplier_profession?: string,
  supplier_vat_number?: string,
  billing_address?: {
    address_line: string,
    city: string,
    state?: string,
    zip: string,
    country: string
  },
  shipping_address?: {
    address_line: string,
    city: string,
    state?: string,
    zip: string,
    country: string,
    branch_code?: number | null
  },
  supplier_contact_person?: string,
  supplier_phone_number?: string,
  supplier_email?: string,
  loading_address?: {
    address_line: string,
    city: string,
    state?: string,
    zip: string,
    country: string,
    branch_code?: number | null
  },
  dispatch_date?: string | null,
  dispatch_time?: string | null,
  vehicle_number?: string,
  move_purpose?: number | null,
  related_entities?: {
    id?: string,
    contact: string,
    contact_display_name: string,
    contact_vat_number?: string,
    type: number,
    billing_address?: {
      address_line: string,
      city: string,
      state?: string,
      zip: string,
      country: string
    }
  }[] | null,
  packages?: {
    id?: string,
    packaging_type: number,
    quantity: number,
    other_packaging_type_title?: string
  }[] | null,
  delivery_notes?: string[],
  currency_code?: string,
  exchange_rate?: string,
  calculator_mode?: string,
  items: {
    id?: string,
    product?: string | null,
    description?: string,
    quantity?: string,
    unit_measure?: number | null,
    unit_value?: string,
    unit_value_gross?: string,
    unit_discount?: string,
    unit_discount_gross?: string,
    unit_discount_percentage?: string,
    unit_discount_mode?: string,
    taxes?: string[],
    unit_total?: string,
    item_net?: string,
    project?: string | null,
    billable?: boolean,
    expense_category: string,
    taric_code?: string,
    warehouse?: string | null,
    skip_stock_update?: boolean
  }[],
  initial?: string,
  net?: string,
  total?: string,
  withholding_taxes?: string[],
  payable?: string,
  paid?: string,
  template?: string | null,
  public_notes?: string,
  trackingcategories?: {
    trackingcategory: string,
    option: string
  }[],
  permalink?: string | null,
  organization?: number,
  created?: string,
  modified?: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | No | — |
| `custom_id` | `string` | No | — |
| `representation` | `string` | No | — |
| `status` | `string` | No | — |
| `draft` | `boolean` | No | — |

### bills_delete

`v11.bills_delete`

Delete a bill.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.elorus.api.v11.bills_delete({
	path: { id: 'demo' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `demo` | `boolean` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
{
  id: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `undefined`


---

### bills_email_create

`v11.bills_email_create`

Send the document by email.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.elorus.api.v11.bills_email_create({
	path: { id: 'demo' },
	body: {
		to: 'someone@example.com',
		cc: [],
		bcc: [],
		subject: 'The email subject',
		message: 'Hello,<br/>please find the PDF attached.',
		attach_pdf: false,
	},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `demo` | `boolean` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
{
  id: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  to: string,
  store_to?: boolean,
  cc: string[],
  bcc: string[],
  reply_to?: string,
  subject: string,
  message: string,
  attach_pdf: boolean,
  sender_name?: string,
  attachments?: string[]
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | No | — |
| `from_email` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `to` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `cc` | `string` | No | — |
| `bcc` | `string` | No | — |
| `reply_to` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `subject` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `message` | `string` | No | — |
| `attachments` | `string` | No | — |
| `attach_pdf` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `status` | `string` | No | — |
| `status_updated` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `sent_via_smtp` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `smtp_error` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `smtp_error_code` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `metadata` | `Record<string, string>` | No | — |
| `created` | `string` | No | — |
| `modified` | `string` | No | — |



---

### bills_email_from_template

`v11.bills_email_from_template`

The subject and message are rendered from the template resolved via templateid, or the organization's default template if not provided.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.elorus.api.v11.bills_email_from_template({
	path: { id: 'demo' },
	body: { to: 'someone@example.com' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `query` | `object` | No | — |
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `demo` | `boolean` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
{
  id: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="query full type">

```ts
{
  template_id?: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  to: string,
  store_to?: boolean,
  cc?: string[],
  bcc?: string[],
  reply_to?: string,
  attach_pdf?: boolean,
  attachments?: string[]
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | No | — |
| `from_email` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `to` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `cc` | `string` | No | — |
| `bcc` | `string` | No | — |
| `reply_to` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `subject` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `message` | `string` | No | — |
| `attachments` | `string` | No | — |
| `attach_pdf` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `status` | `string` | No | — |
| `status_updated` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `sent_via_smtp` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `smtp_error` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `smtp_error_code` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `metadata` | `Record<string, string>` | No | — |
| `created` | `string` | No | — |
| `modified` | `string` | No | — |

### bills_list

`v11.bills_list`

Get the list of bills.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.elorus.api.v11.bills_list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `query` | `object` | No | — |
| `demo` | `boolean` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="query full type">

```ts
{
  ordering?: string,
  search?: string,
  search_fields?: string,
  period_from?: string,
  period_to?: string,
  period?: string,
  status?: string,
  draft?: string,
  pending_approval?: string,
  fpaid?: string,
  is_void?: string,
  overdue?: string,
  supplier?: string,
  currency_code?: string,
  documenttype?: string,
  self_billed?: string,
  sequence?: string,
  custom_id?: string,
  created_after?: string,
  created_before?: string,
  modified_after?: string,
  modified_before?: string,
  modified_period?: string,
  created_period?: string,
  page?: number,
  page_size?: number
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `count` | `number` | Yes | — |
| `next` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `previous` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `results` | `object[]` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="results full type">

```ts
{
  id?: string,
  custom_id?: string,
  representation?: string,
  status?: string,
  draft?: boolean,
  documenttype?: string | null,
  self_billed?: boolean,
  sequence?: string,
  reference?: string,
  date?: string,
  due_date?: string | null,
  branch?: string | null,
  supplier?: string,
  currency_code?: string,
  exchange_rate?: string,
  calculator_mode?: string,
  initial?: string,
  net?: string,
  total?: string,
  payable?: string,
  paid?: string,
  permalink?: string | null,
  organization?: number,
  created?: string,
  modified?: string
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### bills_mark_void

`v11.bills_mark_void`

Mark the document as void or convert it from "void" back to "issued".

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.elorus.api.v11.bills_mark_void({
	path: { id: 'demo' },
	body: {},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `demo` | `boolean` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
{
  id: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  void?: boolean
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `status` | `string` | No | — |

### recurringinvoices_update

`v11.recurringinvoices_update`

When changing the invoice client, all client fields will be updated based on the new contact record, unless you explicitly specify their values.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.elorus.api.v11.recurringinvoices_update({
	path: { id: 'demo' },
	body: {
		end_datetime: '2020-01-01T06:00:00Z',
		client: '3300000040000100',
		items: [],
	},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |

Send the document by email.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.elorus.api.v12.deliverynotes_email_create({
	path: { id: 'demo' },
	body: {
		to: 'someone@example.com',
		cc: [],
		bcc: [],
		subject: 'The email subject',
		message: 'Hello,<br/>please find the PDF attached.',
		attach_pdf: false,
	},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `demo` | `boolean` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
{
  id: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  to: string,
  store_to?: boolean,
  cc: string[],
  bcc: string[],
  reply_to?: string,
  subject: string,
  message: string,
  attach_pdf: boolean,
  sender_name?: string,
  attachments?: string[]
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | No | — |
| `from_email` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `to` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `cc` | `string` | No | — |
| `bcc` | `string` | No | — |
| `reply_to` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `subject` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `message` | `string` | No | — |
| `attachments` | `string` | No | — |
| `attach_pdf` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `status` | `string` | No | — |
| `status_updated` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `sent_via_smtp` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `smtp_error` | `string` | Yes | — |

| `metadata` | `Record<string, string>` | No | — |

| `modified` | `string` | No | — |
